## CELL ALETT-001

UTTERLY FULL PERFECT PROMPT — Aletheia / ALETT-001
Date stamp: 04/09/2026
Source: docs/00_law/Prompt Request Policy.md (canonical template) — generated by prompt_generator.py

1. UTTERLY / FULL DETAIL
Produce an utterly fine-detail Work Order package for everything discussed below: Not yet mapped — establish identity from tree evidence; bind to estate map
Include every fine detail discussed, every fixture, file, and gate. No partial answers, no
summary-only replies. Expand ANY section that needs it; leave nothing implicit.

2. PROOF
Self-KPI inter-section gates; Propups + tests for compliance and anti-fakery (always-firing
require()-style checks; no bare assert). Every claim is tagged [V: <command>, HEAD <sha>] or
[R: <source>, unverified]. No doc-green, no hollow delivery. BLAKE3 seal where ratification applies.

3. STANDARDS STAMP
best case prompt engineering as of 04/09/2026; best case use settings as of 04/09/2026;
best prompt quality standards as of 04/09/2026.

4. MODEL ROUTING
Execution surface (as of 03/09/2026): lh-harness + OpenCode + GLM-5.3-Flash.
Manager reasoning effort high · Executor max · Auditor high (roles per LongHorizon-Harness 0.1.7).
Planning/ratification arcs never run inside an implementer role. OpenCode + /psiforcedb-swarm
(4-person cell: E++T, C++26 Programmer, PsiForceDB Programmer, QC ≥9.5/10) unless the Work
Order fixes a different cell map.

5. EXECUTION SURFACE
lh-harness run --task '@{path}' --agent opencode --model zai/glm-5.3-flash
 --manager-reasoning-effort high --executor-reasoning-effort max --auditor-reasoning-effort high
 --workspace 'C:\McMaker Projects\Projects\Aletheia' --prompt-language en --base-url https://api.z.ai/api/coding/paas/v4
One bounded step per round; max 80 rounds per run; one cell per task.

6. ENGINEERING BAR
C++26 pushed to the envelope of innovation and technical correctness on touched translation
units. MEMO L-021: production-bound work is not validated until it builds AND tests green
under WSL Ubuntu-26.04 g++-16 (both generators tracked: ninja-wsl-gcc + make-wsl-gcc).
Banned dependencies (configure gates FATAL): curl, sqlite3, liboqs, google-benchmark,
system-argon2, OpenSSL. Estate crypto is LFSSL only. PFQL only (no SQL). BLAKE3 primary.
Fail closed always. Owner Axioms 1-9 verbatim.

7. STUB LAW
ZERO STUBS. No permitted stub in this unit. No workarounds, no placeholders, no VOID-tags,
no maybe_unused, no (void) casts, no commented-out code, no forward declaration that is
never defined and linked.

8. LAMIA FABRICA PLUG
Orient ALL design to Lamia Fabrica / PsiForceDB / Aegis / LFSSL infrastructure and
technologies: Estate system (not yet mapped - honest). Zero middleware on critical paths. Estate utilities consumed
in place from tools\tools (never mirrored).

9. QUALITY AIM
World-leading, trend-setting solution for: CODE_STANDARD (Aletheia vertical). Not vague best effort.

10. TRUST MODEL
Zero Trust enforced with new technological gates (Propup, capability, LFSSL wrap, policy IDs)
— not classical middleware trust. Policy IDs printed on failure; fail closed.

11. SECTION DISCIPLINE
One section at a time. Stop at end of each section. Proceed ONLY if Self-KPI is GREEN
and comprehensively applied. No COMPLETE without a green Propup on the real path.
